# What the run could not find: fy2025_runs_datapoints.csv

18658 missing values in 1463 of 1510 companies (built 22 Nov 2026). Not asked for and not applicable rows are counted but are not failures.

## By reason

| Code | Reason | Values | Companies | What to do |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `WEBSITE_NOT_FOUND` | Company website not found | 33 | 1 | Fill in `website` (or `annual_report_url`) in the input and re-run |
| `REPORT_NOT_FOUND` | Annual report not found | 633 | 36 | Put the FY annual report link (PDF, or a gs:// file) in `annual_report_url` and re-run |
| `REMUNERATION_REPORT_NOT_FOUND` | Remuneration report not found | 29 | 29 | Put the remuneration report link in `remuneration_report_url` and re-run with fields=pay |
| `NO_IDENTIFIER` | Needs an LEI or register number | 24 | 24 | Add `lei` or `business_id` to the input: only an official register gives this field |
| `WRONG_YEAR_REPORT` | Values read from an older report | 84 | 84 | Put the FY report link in `annual_report_url` and re-run with --redo |
| `CREDITS_EXHAUSTED` | Firecrawl credits ran out | 33 | 5 | Top up credits (or raise --max-run-credits / --doc-credits) and re-run |
| `DOCUMENT_UNREADABLE` | Document could not be read | 59 | 5 | Scanned or broken file: supply a text PDF, or check the download |
| `NETWORK_ERROR` | Network or site error | 71 | 6 | Re-run; if it repeats, supply the document link |
| `DERIVATION_INPUT_MISSING` | Calculated field: an input is missing | 484 | 473 | Fix the input field it rests on (share price, shares outstanding, business description) |
| `FX_RATE_MISSING` | Calculated field: no exchange rate | 2 | 2 | Re-run the field (the API step gives the ECB rate), or fix the currency unit |
| `STATED_IN_OTHER_FORM` | Stated, but not in the form asked for | 54 | 28 | Accept or review: e.g. a % of salary where an amount is asked |
| `REMUNERATION_REPORT_NOT_READ` | Pay field: only the annual report was read | 7581 | 815 | Supply the remuneration report in `remuneration_report_url` and re-run the field |
| `NOT_DISCLOSED` | Not stated in the documents read | 9571 | 1085 | Review: the documents were read and do not state it; re-run only with a new source |
| `NOT_APPLICABLE` | Does not apply | 7089 | 1406 | - |
| `NOT_COLLECTED` | Not asked for in this run | 20631 | 913 | - |
